- Window: PRs merged into docs/ranking-1-release-line after #1625 closed (2026-09-29T02:27:33Z), up to the frozen tip. Each section names the revision it was checked against: the first sweep dc44ebc7be5393edf6023507ea986e5b0853314f, the top-up 620bca303, delta 1 8b9929924 (47f0aade6 adds only CI), delta 2 598b3650e, deltas 3 and 3b 68d44f5c3. Top-up section at the end covers PRs merged after #2428 plus 30 earlier ones.
- Sources: inline review comments from three automated reviewers (Copilot, codex, code scanning). Review-summary bodies carry no separate file:line findings.
- Every row checked against the code at its section's revision (named above). Fix SHAs for squash-merged PRs are the squash commit. A Done ticket alone did not make a row FIXED: rows whose claim still holds at the tip keep their ticket. The final sweep at the frozen tip re-runs `scripts/release/botreview_sweep.py`, which exits 1 while any bot thread lacks its own link, and rechecks every PENDING row.

| #2847 | [src/gateway/meta_mcp/invoke/relay.rs](https://github.com/MikkoParkkola/mcp-gateway/pull/2847#discussion_r4173787136) | Single receipt from composite call treated as single-target | REAL-OPEN (LOW) | `restage_if_changed` still infers single-target from `[one]` staged receipt (src/gateway/meta_mcp/invoke/relay.rs:585) and digests the whole delivered result under that step's target (:586-590), so a redacted playbook with one staged step over-attributes text. Effect is false relay matches (stricter), not a bypass. -> MIK-7939 (v4.0.0) |
| #2847 | [src/gateway/meta_mcp/invoke/relay.rs](https://github.com/MikkoParkkola/mcp-gateway/pull/2847#discussion_r4175440700) | Native one-block JSON text mistaken for invoke wrapper | FIXED | `delivered_value` now decodes only when the text is exactly the pretty-printed JSON object it parses to (src/gateway/meta_mcp/invoke/audit.rs:346-355), so ordinary compact or field-shuffled JSON text is recorded as delivered. |
| #2847 | [src/gateway/meta_mcp/task_replay.rs:134](https://github.com/MikkoParkkola/mcp-gateway/pull/2847#discussion_r4175521673) | Gateway-authored fail-closed error recorded as backend output | FIXED | A failed task stages a receipt only via `backend_error()`, which requires `error_author == Some(ErrorAuthor::Peer)` (src/gateway/task_service/record.rs:391; src/gateway/meta_mcp/task_replay.rs:131-140), so gateway-written errors receipt nothing (MIK-7887). |
| #2847 | [scripts/ci/check-loc-ceiling.sh:16](https://github.com/MikkoParkkola/mcp-gateway/pull/2847#discussion_r4175562712) | LOC ceiling lowered below actual breach count | FIXED | At the tip `CEILING=21` (scripts/ci/check-loc-ceiling.sh:16) and src/ has exactly 21 non-test .rs files over 800 lines (counted with fd + wc), so the script passes. |

| #2910 | [docs/release/v4.0.0-gate-day-procedure.md](https://github.com/MikkoParkkola/mcp-gateway/pull/2910#discussion_r4177959118) | Key-server annotation says "Operator action: none" | FIXED | docs/release/v4.0.0-gate-day-procedure.md:177 now tells operators to check each key-server token issued with an empty backends or tools list against its policy rule and revoke live mis-scoped tokens, matching changelog.d/757.security.md:2. | #2920 | [src/gateway/router/handlers.rs:1981](https://github.com/MikkoParkkola/mcp-gateway/pull/2920#discussion_r4178244555) | Only gateway_invoke decoded; other wrapped meta-tools fingerprint JSON text | REAL-OPEN (LOW, SECURITY) | src/gateway/router/handlers.rs:1978-1982 and src/gateway/server/mod.rs:3072-3076 still pick InvokeWrapped only when `external_tool == "gateway_invoke"`, while every synchronous meta-tool success is wrapped by wrap_tool_success (src/gateway/meta_mcp/response_security.rs:533-545), so a single-target gateway_execute receipt digests the escaped outer text. -> MIK-7939 (v4.0.0) |
| #2920 | [src/gateway/meta_mcp/invoke/relay.rs](https://github.com/MikkoParkkola/mcp-gateway/pull/2920#discussion_r4178263955) | Rebuild gated on one staged receipt, not single-target call | REAL-OPEN (LOW, SECURITY) | src/gateway/meta_mcp/invoke/relay_delivered.rs:71-73 still rebuilds whenever exactly one receipt is staged (`let [one] = ...`); no target-cardinality input, so a playbook with one successful step has its receipt replaced by a digest of the whole aggregate result. -> MIK-7939 (v4.0.0) |
| #2920 | [src/gateway/router/handlers.rs:1985](https://github.com/MikkoParkkola/mcp-gateway/pull/2920#discussion_r4178348138) | tasks/get rebuild digests the whole task envelope | REAL-OPEN (LOW, SECURITY) | For tasks/get and tasks/update, external_tool is the method (src/gateway/router/handlers.rs:1024-1028), so the rebuild at 1978-1985 runs Literal over the full task wire object, replacing the backend-only receipt staged by stage_stored_receipt (src/gateway/meta_mcp/task_replay.rs:119-123); no nested result/error/inputRequests selection. -> MIK-7939 (v4.0.0) |
| #2920 | [src/gateway/task_service/execution/worker.rs:553](https://github.com/MikkoParkkola/mcp-gateway/pull/2920#discussion_r4178394845) | Task rebuild skips as_result_object normalization | REAL-OPEN (LOW, SECURITY) | src/gateway/task_service/execution/worker.rs:546-552 rebuilds from `backend_output(result)` only, which strips a _meta key (src/gateway/task_service/execution/settlement.rs:64-69) but not the as_result_object wrapping (71-80) that settlement stores, so a primitive result's receipt differs from the stored `to_string()` text. -> MIK-7939 (v4.0.0) |
| #2920 | [src/gateway/proxy.rs:578](https://github.com/MikkoParkkola/mcp-gateway/pull/2920#discussion_r4178451762) | Prompt receipt committed before the SSE gate emits | REAL-OPEN (LOW, SECURITY) | src/gateway/proxy.rs:570-578 still calls `commit.commit()` right after `send_to_session` queues the frame, before the stream's cross-tenant audit can withhold it, so a withheld prompt can still record a receipt. -> MIK-7939 (v4.0.0) |
| #2920 | [src/gateway/meta_mcp/invoke/relay_delivered.rs:125](https://github.com/MikkoParkkola/mcp-gateway/pull/2920#discussion_r4178538751) | Primitive gateway_invoke wrappers not decoded before rebuild | REAL-OPEN (LOW, SECURITY) | receipt_copy delegates to delivered_value (src/gateway/meta_mcp/invoke/relay_delivered.rs:223-225), which still accepts only `decoded.is_object()` (src/gateway/meta_mcp/invoke/audit.rs:353-355), so a primitive invoke result is digested as its pretty-JSON wrapper. -> MIK-7939 (v4.0.0) |
| #2920 | [src/gateway/meta_mcp/invoke/relay_delivered.rs:113](https://github.com/MikkoParkkola/mcp-gateway/pull/2920#discussion_r4178693729) | Gateway _signature left in rebuilt receipts | REAL-OPEN (LOW, SECURITY) | receipt_copy (src/gateway/meta_mcp/invoke/relay_delivered.rs:208-228) strips the chain, clamps scope and removes the modern serverInfo, but never removes a top-level `_signature`; nothing at the tip excludes the gateway-written signature before the HTTP (handlers.rs:1985) or stdio (server/mod.rs:3077) rebuild. -> MIK-7939 (v4.0.0) |
